    import pytest
    
    from tests.e2e.conftest import Cli, make_cli
    
    pytestmark = pytest.mark.slow
    
    STARTUP_BUDGET_S = 0.300
    INGEST_BUDGET_S = 5.0
    
    
    def median_runtime(cli: Cli, *args: str, runs: int = 5) -> float:
        cli(*args)  # warm-up
        times = []
        for _ in range(runs):
            start = time.perf_counter()
            cli(*args)
            times.append(time.perf_counter() - start)
        return statistics.median(times)
    
    
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("args", [("--help",), ("list",), ("done", "zzzz")], ids=" ".join)
    def test_TC_M12_12_startup_under_300ms(ingested: Cli, args: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
        elapsed = median_runtime(ingested, *args)
        assert elapsed < STARTUP_BUDGET_S, f"{' '.join(args)}: median {elapsed * 1000:.0f} ms"
    
    
    def test_TC_M12_13_sample_ingest_within_budget(tmp_path: Path, expected: object) -> None:
        cli = make_cli(tmp_path / "cli")
        start = time.perf_counter()
        r = cli("ingest", "notes")
        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start
        assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
        if elapsed > INGEST_BUDGET_S:
            warnings.warn(f"ingest took {elapsed:.1f} s (budget {INGEST_BUDGET_S} s)", stacklevel=1)
        assert elapsed < 2 * INGEST_BUDGET_S
    EOF
    cat > tests/e2e/test_release.py <<'EOF'
    """M12 release checks: clean-venv wheel install (TC-M12-14) and traceability (TC-M12-15)."""
    
    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import importlib.util
    import shutil
    import subprocess
    import sys
    from pathlib import Path
    from types import ModuleType
    
    import pytest
    
    from actiontrack import __version__
    from tests.e2e.conftest import REPO
    
    pytestmark = pytest.mark.slow
    
    TRACE_CHECK = REPO / "scripts" / "trace_check.py"
    
    
    def load_trace_check() -> ModuleType:
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("trace_check", TRACE_CHECK)
        assert spec is not None and spec.loader is not None
        module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
        return module
    
    
    def bin_dir(venv: Path) -> Path:
        return venv / ("Scripts" if sys.platform == "win32" else "bin")
    
    
    def test_TC_M12_14_wheel_installs_into_a_clean_venv(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        uv = shutil.which("uv")
        if uv is None:
            pytest.skip("uv is not on PATH")
        dist, venv = tmp_path / "dist", tmp_path / "venv"
        subprocess.run([uv, "build", "--wheel", "--out-dir", str(dist)], cwd=REPO, check=True)
        (wheel,) = dist.glob("actiontrack-*.whl")
        subprocess.run([uv, "venv", "--seed", str(venv)], check=True)
        python = bin_dir(venv) / ("python.exe" if sys.platform == "win32" else "python")
        subprocess.run([uv, "pip", "install", "--python", str(python), str(wheel)], check=True)
        r = subprocess.run(
            [str(bin_dir(venv) / "actiontrack"), "--version"], capture_output=True, text=True
        )
        assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
        assert r.stdout == f"actiontrack {__version__}\n"
    
    
    def test_TC_M12_15_trace_check_passes() -> None:
        r = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, str(TRACE_CHECK)], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8"
        )
        assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
    
    
    def fake_repo(root: Path, *, owners: str, tests: str) -> Path:
        (root / "spec/modules/MA").mkdir(parents=True)
        (root / "spec/requirements.md").write_text(
            "## 18. Requirement Index\n| REQ-X-01 | a |\n| REQ-ACC-01..02 | b |\n## 19. Future\n",
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        (root / "spec/plan.md").write_text(
            f"## 2. Modules\n| ID | Owned |\n|---|---|\n{owners}\n## 3. Next\n", encoding="utf-8"
        )
        (root / "spec/modules/MA/tests.md").write_text(tests, encoding="utf-8")
        return root
    
    
    def test_trace_check_expands_ranges(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        root = fake_repo(
            tmp_path,
            owners="| [M1](x) | X-01 |\n| [M2](y) | ACC-01..02 |",
            tests="REQ-X-01 REQ-ACC-01, REQ-ACC-02",
        )
        assert load_trace_check().check(root) == []
    
    
    def test_trace_check_reports_untested_and_double_owned(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        root = fake_repo(
            tmp_path,
            owners="| [M1](x) | X-01, ACC-01..02 |\n| [M2](y) | ACC-02, Y-09 |",
            tests="REQ-X-01 REQ-ACC-01",
        )
        assert load_trace_check().check(root) == [
            "REQ-ACC-02: no test case in spec/modules/*/tests.md",
            "REQ-ACC-02: owned by 2 modules ['M1', 'M2'] in plan §2",
            "REQ-Y-09: owned in plan §2 but not in root §18",
        ]
    EOF

    if heading_list is None and _ATTENDEES_HEADING.match(line):
                heading_list = "before"
    
        return scan
    
    
    def _title(fm_title: Any, h1: str | None) -> str | None:
        if fm_title is not None and str(fm_title).strip():
            return str(fm_title).strip()
        if h1 is None:
            return None
        found = _H1_DATE.search(h1)
        if found is None:
            title = h1.strip(_TITLE_SEPARATORS)
        else:
            left = h1[: found.start()].rstrip(_TITLE_SEPARATORS)
            right = h1[found.end() :].lstrip(_TITLE_SEPARATORS)
            title = " ".join(part for part in (left, right) if part).strip(_TITLE_SEPARATORS)
        return title or None
    
    
    
    def test_h1_without_year_is_unparseable(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        skip = load_skip(
            write(tmp_path, "# Sync — 18 Nov\nAttendees: Priya\n"), SkipReason.UNPARSEABLE_DATE
        )
        assert "18 Nov" in skip.detail

            ("# Sync: 18th November 2026 - follow-up", "Sync follow-up"),
            ("# Nov 18, 2026: Kickoff", "Kickoff"),
            ("# Kickoff", "Kickoff"),
            ("# 2026-11-18", None),
        ],
    )

        note = f"{h1}\nDate: 2026-11-18\nAttendees: Priya\n"
        assert load_ok(write(tmp_path, note)).title == title

      "file_path": "C:\\Users\\Coditas-Admin\\Desktop\\actionTracker\\README.md",
      "content": "# actiontrack\n\nExtract, track and follow up on action items from Markdown meeting notes. `actiontrack` reads checkbox lines such as\n`- [ ] Priya to send the revised quote to Acme by Friday`, works out the owner and due date, merges duplicates across\nmeetings, and drafts follow-up emails in Gmail.\n\n**It never sends email.** It only creates drafts, which you review and send yourself (see [No-send guarantee](#no-send-guarantee)).\n\nThe full specification is in [spec/requirements.md](spec/requirements.md).\n\n## Install\n\nPython 3.11 or later. Install it as an isolated tool:\n\n```sh\nuv tool install .        # or: pipx install .\nactiontrack --version\n```\n\nFor development:\n\n```sh\nuv sync\nuv run actiontrack --help\n```\n\n## Quickstart\n\n```sh\nactiontrack ingest notes/                  # read every *.md note under notes/\nactiontrack list                           # open items, overdue first\nactiontrack done 17770a                    # mark one done (any unique prefix of 4+ chars)\nactiontrack followup \"Priya Shah\" --dry-run   # preview the follow-up email\nactiontrack followup \"Priya Shah\"             # create it as a Gmail draft\n```\n\nA note needs a meeting date and attendees. Both layouts work:\n\n```markdown\n# Meeting: Quote portal weekly\nDate: 2026-09-08\nAttendees: Priya Nair, Rahul Mehta <rahul.mehta@coditas.com>\n\n## Action items\n- [ ] Priya N. to send revised quote to Acme by Friday\n- [x] Rahul to set up the staging environment\n```\n\n```markdown\n---\ntitle: Team standup\ndate: 2026-09-16\nattendees: [Anita Desai, Lena Fischer]\n---\n- [ ] Lena to draft the case study outline by 2026-09-21\n```\n\n## Commands\n\nGlobal options: `--db PATH`, `--config PATH`, `-v/-vv`, `-q`, `--no-color`, `--debug`, `--version`, `-h/--help`.\nEvery command's `--help` has examples.\n\n### `ingest <folder>`\n\nReads every `*.md` note (recursively; `--no-recursive` for one level), extracts the action items and merges duplicates.\nUnchanged files are skipped, so re-running is safe. Broken notes are listed and skipped, and the run carries on.\n\n```console\n$ actiontrack ingest notes/\nScanned 35 files: 30 ingested, 0 unchanged, 5 skipped, 0 removed\nItems: 111 sources → 88 unique (23 exact merges, 0 fuzzy merges)\nFlags: 6 unassigned, 5 ambiguous_owner, 5 owner_not_attendee, 1 multi_owner, 19 no_due, 4 unparsed_due\nSkipped:\n  notes/2026-09-09-notes-final-v2.md   empty_file: file is empty\n  notes/2026-09-23-standup.md          missing_attendees: no attendees listed\n  notes/2026-10-03-sales-pipeline.md   unparseable_date: cannot parse meeting date 'sometime last week'\n  notes/scratch.md                     binary_file: file contains NUL bytes\n  notes/untitled.md                    missing_date: no meeting date in frontmatter, Date: line or H1\n```\n\nOptions: `--no-fuzzy` (exact merges only, for this run), `--report-json PATH` (the report as JSON).\n\n### `list`\n\n```console\n$ actiontrack list --owner \"Priya Shah\"\n┌────────┬────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┬────────────┬───────────┬─────┐\n│ ID     │ Owner      │ Task                                     │ Due        │ Status    │ Src │\n├────────┼────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┼────────────┼───────────┼─────┤\n│ 17770a │ Priya Shah │ draft the test plan for invoice rounding │ 2026-09-11 │ ⚠ overdue │ 1   │\n│ 267499 │ Priya Shah │ set up the test data                     │ —          │ open      │ 1   │\n└────────┴────────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────┴────────────┴───────────┴─────┘\n```\n\n| Option | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `--owner TEXT` | Person key, email, full name or first name. An ambiguous first name lists the candidates (exit 2) |\n| `--status open\\|done\\|overdue` | `open` (the default) includes overdue items |\n| `--due-before DATE` | e.g. `2026-12-01` or `friday` |\n| `--unassigned` | Items with no owner |\n| `--flag NAME` | e.g. `ambiguous_owner`, `no_due`. Orphaned items (their note line was removed) show only with `--flag orphaned` |\n| `--json` | Machine-readable output with a `schema_version`. stdout carries only the JSON |\n\nOverdue rows are red **and** read `⚠ overdue`, so colour is never the only signal.\n\n### `done <id>`\n\n```console\n$ actiontrack done 1777\n✓ 17770a  draft the test plan for invoice rounding  (Priya Shah)\n```\n\nA done item stays done when its note is re-ingested.\n\n### `followup <owner>`\n\nDrafts one email to the owner listing their overdue and open items, each with its due date, source meeting and ID.\nThe recipient is the attendee's email, or `firstname.lastname@<email.domain>` derived from the full name.\n\n```console\n$ actiontrack followup \"Priya Shah\" --dry-run\nDry run: no draft created.\nTo: priya.shah@coditas.com\nSubject: Follow-up: your open action items (2 open, 5 overdue)\n\nHi Priya,\n...\n```\n\nWithout `--dry-run` it creates the Gmail draft and prints its link. If the Gmail MCP server can't be reached, the\nemail is saved to the outbox folder (`<data dir>/outbox/*.eml`) and the command exits 3.\n\n### `config show`\n\nPrints each effective setting and where it came from (`flag`, `env`, `file` or `default`). `--json` is supported.\n\n## Configuration\n\nPrecedence: command-line flags > environment variables > config file > defaults.\n\n| Setting | Config file | Environment | Default |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Database | `db_path` | `ACTIONTRACK_DB` (or `--db`) | `<data dir>/actiontrack.db` |\n| Outbox folder | `outbox_dir` | — | `<data dir>/outbox` |\n| Email domain | `[email] domain` | `ACTIONTRACK_EMAIL_DOMAIN` | `coditas.com` |\n| Sign-off | `[email] sign_off` | — | `Thanks` |\n| Fuzzy merging | `[dedup] fuzzy_enabled`, `fuzzy_threshold` | `ACTIONTRACK_FUZZY_THRESHOLD` | `true`, `88` |\n| Numeric dates | `[parsing] date_order` | `ACTIONTRACK_DATE_ORDER` | `DMY` |\n| \"Today\" (tests, demos) | — | `ACTIONTRACK_TODAY=2026-11-25` | the local date |\n\nThe config file is `config.toml` in the OS config folder (`%APPDATA%\\actiontrack\\` on Windows, `~/.config/actiontrack/`\non Linux, `~/Library/Application Support/actiontrack/` on macOS). The data dir is the OS data folder for `actiontrack`.\n\n```toml\n[email]\ndomain = \"coditas.com\"\nsign_off = \"Thanks,\\nMohit\"\n\n[dedup]\nfuzzy_threshold = 88\n```\n\n### Gmail MCP\n\n`followup` creates drafts through a Gmail MCP server's `create_draft` tool. The CLI stores no Google credentials: point\nit at an endpoint that is already authenticated, either a local stdio server or an HTTP URL.\n\n```toml\n[gmail_mcp]\ntransport = \"stdio\"                 # or \"http\"\ncommand = \"npx\"                     # stdio: how to launch the server\nargs = [\"-y\", \"<gmail-mcp-server>\"]\n# url = \"http://localhost:8080/mcp\" # http: the server URL\ndraft_tool = \"create_draft\"\nto_as_list = true                   # the Gmail connector takes `to` as a list\n# arg_names = { to = \"to\", subject = \"subject\", body = \"body\" }   # if the server names them differently\ntimeout_s = 30\n```\n\nBefore calling the tool, `actiontrack` checks that the server lists `draft_tool` and that its input schema accepts the\narguments it sends.\n\n#### Manual smoke test\n\nRun this once with the real server configured, before a release:\n\n1. Ingest notes in which you are an owner, then run `actiontrack followup \"<your name>\" --dry-run` and check the email.\n2. Run `actiontrack followup \"<your name>\"`. It prints a draft link and exits 0.\n3. In Gmail, open **Drafts**: the draft is there, addressed to you. Check **Sent**: nothing was sent.\n\n## Exit codes\n\n| Code | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `0` | Success, including an ingest that skipped broken notes, or output cut short by a closed pipe (`\\| head`) |\n| `1` | Fatal error (folder missing, database not writable) |\n| `2` | User input error (unknown or ambiguous ID or owner, bad filter, bad config) |\n| `3` | The Gmail MCP server failed. The email was saved to the outbox |\n| `130` | Interrupted (Ctrl-C). The current database transaction is rolled back |\n\nErrors are one short `Error:` line on stderr. `--debug` (or `ACTIONTRACK_DEBUG=1`) adds the Python traceback.\n\n## No-send guarantee\n\n- The MCP adapter exposes a single `create_draft(to, subject, body)` method. No generic tool call is reachable from the\n  rest of the code.\n- A hard-coded allowlist containing only `create_draft` is checked before every tool call. Any other tool name raises\n  `ForbiddenToolError`.\n- The tests run against fake servers that also offer `send_message`, `reply` and `forward`, and assert those are never called.\n\n## Development\n\n```sh\nuv sync\nuv run ruff check . && uv run ruff format --check .\nuv run mypy\nuv run pytest                     # fast tests\nuv run pytest -m slow             # end-to-end and performance tests on notes/\nuv run python scripts/trace_check.py\n```\n\nThe work is spec-driven: see [spec/plan.md](spec/plan.md) for the modules, the status board and the decisions log.\n"
    }

    M12: e2e acceptance suite on the sample notes, trace check, README, CI

The first CI run on [PR #12](https://github.com/mohit-gupta-coditas/actiontrack-py/pull/12) failed on all six jobs, because of a test bug rather than a product bug. I've pushed a fix (`6610f61`), and the new run is going now.

GitHub Actions sets `GITHUB_ACTIONS`, which makes Typer add colour codes to `--help` output, and those codes broke up the `--dry-run` text the test looks for. It hadn't shown up before because M12 adds the repo's first CI.
- **Fix:** the test strips colour codes before checking. The CLI itself is unchanged.

    First release of `actiontrack`: extract, track and follow up on action items from Markdown meeting notes.
    
    ## Commands
    - `ingest <folder>`: reads every `*.md` note, extracts checkbox action items (owner, task, due date), merges duplicates across meetings, and reports and skips broken notes. Re-running only processes changed files.
    - `list`: open, overdue or done items, filtered by owner, due date, flag or unassigned; also as a table or `--json`.
    - `done <id>`: marks an item done by any unique 4+ character ID prefix. It stays done across re-ingests.
    - `followup <owner>`: drafts one follow-up email per owner through a Gmail MCP server, with `--dry-run` to preview. **It never sends**: only `create_draft` is allowed.
    - `config show`: the effective settings and where each came from.
    
    ## Install
    ```sh
    uv tool install actiontrack-0.1.0-py3-none-any.whl   # or: pipx install …
    ```
    Python 3.11+. See the README for configuration, Gmail MCP setup and exit codes.
    
    ## Quality
    - Spec-driven: 13 modules (M0–M12), every requirement traced to a test case (`scripts/trace_check.py`).
    - Acceptance tested end to end on a 37-file sample of real-world notes, including malformed ones.
    - CI: Python 3.11–3.13 on ubuntu and windows. Start-up under 300 ms on Linux.

       --due-before <date>. Overdu
    3. actiontrack done <id> marks an item as done.

A previous session used an export script for this format. I'll write a compatible one in the scratchpad and run it on this session's transcript (`fc60775a…`).

  [tool: Bash] [Export this session's transcript to conv13.txt]
  $ SP="C:/Users/CODITA~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce/scratchpad"; mkdir -p "$SP"; cat > "$SP/export.py" <<'EOF'
    import json, re, sys
    
    src, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    SEP = "#" * 100
    STRIP = re.compile(r"<(system-reminder|ide_opened_file|ide_selection)>.*?</\1>", re.S)
    
    
    def indent(text, pad="    "):
        return "\n".join(pad + line for line in text.splitlines())
    
    
    def clean(text):
        return STRIP.sub("", text).strip()
    
    
    def tool_desc(b):
        name, inp = b.get("name", "?"), b.get("input", {}) or {}
        head = f"  [tool: {name}]" + (f" [{inp['description']}]" if inp.get("description") else "")
        if "command" in inp:
            cmd = inp["command"].splitlines() or [""]
            return "\n".join([head, "  $ " + cmd[0], *("    " + c for c in cmd[1:])])
        body = {k: v for k, v in inp.items() if k != "description"}
        return head + "\n" + indent(json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
    
    
    def result_text(b):
        c = b.get("content")
        if isinstance(c, list):
            c = "\n".join(x.get("text", "") for x in c if isinstance(x, dict) and x.get("type") == "text")
        return clean(c or "")
    
    
    lines = [
        "actiontrack — Conversation 13 (M12 end-to-end, performance & release; v0.1.0)",
        "Session: fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce",
        "Exported: 2026-11-26",
        "Note: internal reasoning and system reminders omitted; full file contents written by tools live in the repo.",
        "=" * 100,
        "",
    ]
    last_role = None
    for raw in open(src, encoding="utf-8"):
        e = json.loads(raw)
        if e.get("type") not in ("user", "assistant") or e.get("isMeta") or e.get("isSidechain"):
            continue
        content = e["message"].get("content")
        if isinstance(content, str):
            content = [{"type": "text", "text": content}]
        for b in content:
            t = b.get("type")
            if e["type"] == "user" and t == "text":
                text = clean(b["text"])
                if not text or text.startswith("<command-") or text.startswith("<local-command"):
                    continue
                lines += ["", SEP, "USER:", text, ""]
                last_role = "user"
            elif t == "text":
                if not b["text"].strip():
                    continue
                if last_role != "claude":
                    lines.append("CLAUDE:")
                lines += [b["text"].strip(), ""]
                last_role = "claude"
            elif t == "tool_use":
                if last_role != "claude":
                    lines.append("CLAUDE:")
                lines.append(tool_desc(b))
                last_role = "claude"
            elif t == "tool_result":
                r = result_text(b)
                lines += ["  [result]:" + (" (error)" if b.get("is_error") else ""), indent(r or "(no output)"), ""]
    open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    EOF
    python -I "$SP/export.py" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/.claude/projects/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce.jsonl" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations/conv13.txt" && cd "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations" && wc -lc conv13.txt && grep -n -A1 "^USER:" conv13.txt
  [result]:
    5890 371327 conv13.txt
    9:USER:
    10-work on M12 module , if there are any gaps use the ask user question tool wiht recommendation
    Binary file conv13.txt matches

The export worked, but grep calls the file binary: raw bytes from `scratch.md` (dumped while I was reading the notes) got into a tool result. Replacing control characters and re-exporting:

  [tool: Bash] [Sanitize control bytes and re-export]
  $ SP="C:/Users/CODITA~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce/scratchpad"; python -I - "$SP/export.py" <<'PY'
    import sys; p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p,encoding="utf-8").read()
    s=s.replace('open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\\n".join(lines) + "\\n")',
     'text = re.sub(r"[\\x00-\\x08\\x0b-\\x1f\\x7f\\ufffd]", "\\u00b7", "\\n".join(lines) + "\\n")\nopen(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(text)')
    open(p,"w",encoding="utf-8").write(s)
    PY
    tail -2 "$SP/export.py"; python -I "$SP/export.py" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/.claude/projects/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce.jsonl" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations/conv13.txt" && cd "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations" && file conv13.txt && wc -lc conv13.txt && grep -n -A1 "^USER:" conv13.txt
  [result]:
    lines += ["  [result]:" + (" (error)" if b.get("is_error") else ""), indent(r or "(no output)"), ""]
    open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8").write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    conv13.txt: Unicode text, UTF-8 text, with very long lines (420), with CRLF line terminators
      5906 372702 conv13.txt
    9:USER:
    10-work on M12 module , if there are any gaps use the ask user question tool wiht recommendation
    Binary file conv13.txt matches

My patch to the script didn't apply because the string didn't match. Rewriting the last line directly:

  [tool: Bash] [Fix sanitizer and re-export the conversation]
  $ SP="C:/Users/CODITA~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce/scratchpad"; sed -i '$d' "$SP/export.py" && cat >> "$SP/export.py" <<'EOF'
    text = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")
    text = re.sub(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x1f\x7f\ufffd]", "\u00b7", text)  # binary bytes from the notes dump
    open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(text)
    EOF
    python -I "$SP/export.py" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/.claude/projects/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce.jsonl" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations/conv13.txt" && cd "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations" && file conv13.txt && wc -lc conv13.txt && grep -n -A1 "^USER:" conv13.txt; grep -c "^CLAUDE:" conv13.txt; grep -c "\[tool:" conv13.txt; tail -12 conv13.txt

    My patch to the script didn't apply because the string didn't match. Rewriting the last line directly:
    
      [tool: Bash] [Fix sanitizer and re-export the conversation]
      $ SP="C:/Users/CODITA~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce/scratchpad"; sed -i '$d' "$SP/export.py" && cat >> "$SP/export.py" <<'EOF'
        text = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
        text = text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")
        text = re.sub(r"[\x00-\x08\x0b-\x1f\x7f\ufffd]", "\u00b7", text)  # binary bytes from the notes dump
        open(out, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n").write(text)
        EOF
        python -I "$SP/export.py" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/.claude/projects/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce.jsonl" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations/conv13.txt" && cd "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations" && file conv13.txt && wc -lc conv13.txt && grep -n -A1 "^USER:" conv13.txt; grep -c "^CLAUDE:" conv13.txt; grep -c "\[tool:" conv13.txt; tail -12 conv13.txt

  [tool: Bash] [Relabel task notifications and re-export]
  $ SP="C:/Users/CODITA~1/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce/scratchpad"; python -I - "$SP/export.py" <<'PY'
    import sys; p=sys.argv[1]; s=open(p,encoding="utf-8").read()
    old='''            lines += ["", SEP, "USER:", text, ""]
                last_role = "user"'''
    new='''            if text.startswith("<task-notification>"):
                    m = re.search(r"<summary>(.*?)</summary>", text, re.S)
                    lines += ["", "[background task finished: " + (m[1].strip() if m else "?") + "]", ""]
                    last_role = "system"
                    continue
                lines += ["", SEP, "USER:", text, ""]
                last_role = "user"'''
    assert s.count(old)==1; open(p,"w",encoding="utf-8").write(s.replace(old,new))
    PY
    python -I "$SP/export.py" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/.claude/projects/c--Users-Coditas-Admin-Desktop-actionTracker/fc60775a-5ec3-4fb9-ab44-e0af09b2fdce.jsonl" "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations/conv13.txt" && cd "C:/Users/Coditas-Admin/Desktop/actionTracker/conversations" && file conv13.txt && wc -lc conv13.txt && grep -n -A1 "^USER:\|^\[background" conv13.txt | grep -v "^--"
